#        TEST GENERATION BEGINS (DEEPMOSA + DeepSeek t=0.8)        #
####################################################################


# Prompt used at query #1
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import typing as t
import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib
    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also
    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d


class URLSafeSerializer(URLSafeSerializerMixin, Serializer[str]):
    """Works like :class:`.Serializer` but dumps and loads into a URL
    safe string consisting of the upper and lowercase character of the
    alphabet as well as ``'_'``, ``'-'`` and ``'.'``.
    """


class URLSafeTimedSerializer(URLSafeSerializerMixin, TimedSerializer[str]):
    """Works like :class:`.TimedSerializer` but dumps and loads into a
    URL safe string consisting of the upper and lowercase character of
    the alphabet as well as ``'_'``, ``'-'`` and ``'.'``.
    """

Write unit test with pytest for method load_payload of class URLSafeSerializerMixin with the following signature: `def test_URLSafeSerializerMixin_load_payload():`

                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

from __future__ import annotations

import typing as t
import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib
    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also
    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also

# Prompt used at query #74

from __future__ import annotations

import typing as t
import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib
    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also
    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d


class URLSafeSerializer(URLSafeSerializerMixin, Serializer[str]):
    """Works like :class:`.Serializer` but dumps and loads into a URL
    safe string consisting of the upper and lowercase character of the
    alphabet as well as ``'_'``, ``'-'`` and ``'.'``.
    """


class URLSafeTimedSerializer(URLSafeSerializerMixin, TimedSerializer[str]):
    """Works like :class:`.TimedSerializer` but dumps and loads into a
    URL safe string consisting of the upper and lowercase character of
    the alphabet as well as ``'_'``, ``'-'`` and ``'.'``.
    """

Write unit test with pytest for method dump_payload of class URLSafeSerializerMixin with the following signature: `def test_URLSafeSerializerMixin_dump_payload():`

from __future__ import annotations

from __future__ import annotations

import typing as t
import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib
    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also
    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

# Prompt used at query #116
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import typing as t
import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib
    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also
    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:

            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

            is_compressed = True

            base64d = b"." + base64d

import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib
    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also
    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d


class URLSafeSerializer(URLSafeSerializerMixin, Serializer[str]):
    """Works like :class:`.Serializer` but dumps and loads into a URL
    safe string consisting of the upper and lowercase character of the
    alphabet as well as ``'_'``, ``'-'`` and ``'.'``.
    """


class URLSafeTimedSerializer(URLSafeSerializerMixin, TimedSerializer[str]):
    """Works like :class:`.TimedSerializer` but dumps and loads into a
    URL safe string consisting of the upper and lowercase character of
    the alphabet as well as ``'_'``, ``'-'`` and ``'.'``.
    """

Write unit test with pytest for method load_payload of class URLSafeSerializerMixin with the following signature: `def test_URLSafeSerializerMixin_load_payload():`

                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

from __future__ import annotations

import typing as t
import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib

        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:

            decompress = True

        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

# Prompt used at query #61
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import typing as t
import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib
    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also
    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d


class URLSafeSerializer(URLSafeSerializerMixin, Serializer[str]):
    """Works like :class:`.Serializer` but dumps and loads into a URL
    safe string consisting of the upper and lowercase character of the
    alphabet as well as ``'_'``, ``'-'`` and ``'.'``.
    """


class URLSafeTimedSerializer(URLSafeSerializerMixin, TimedSerializer[str]):
    """Works like :class:`.TimedSerializer` but dumps and loads into a
    URL safe string consisting of the upper and lowercase character of
    the alphabet as well as ``'_'``, ``'-'`` and ``'.'``.
    """

Write unit test with pytest for method load_payload of class URLSafeSerializerMixin with the following signature: `def test_URLSafeSerializerMixin_load_payload():`

from __future__ import annotations

from __future__ import annotations

import typing as t
import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib
    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also
    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

# Prompt used at query #103
#--------------------------

from __future__ import annotations

import typing as t
import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib
    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also
    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:

            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,

            is_compressed = True

            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d

import zlib

from ._json import _CompactJSON
from .encoding import base64_decode
from .encoding import base64_encode
from .exc import BadPayload
from .serializer import _PDataSerializer
from .serializer import Serializer
from .timed import TimedSerializer


class URLSafeSerializerMixin(Serializer[str]):
    """Mixed in with a regular serializer it will attempt to zlib
    compress the string to make it shorter if necessary. It will also
    base64 encode the string so that it can safely be placed in a URL.
    """

    default_serializer: _PDataSerializer[str] = _CompactJSON

    def load_payload(
        self,
        payload: bytes,
        *args: t.Any,
        serializer: t.Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: t.Any,
    ) -> t.Any:
        decompress = False

        if payload.startswith(b"."):
            payload = payload[1:]
            decompress = True

        try:
            json = base64_decode(payload)
        except Exception as e:
            raise BadPayload(
                "Could not base64 decode the payload because of an exception",
                original_error=e,
            ) from e

        if decompress:
            try:
                json = zlib.decompress(json)
            except Exception as e:
                raise BadPayload(
                    "Could not zlib decompress the payload before decoding the payload",
                    original_error=e,
                ) from e

        return super().load_payload(json, *args, **kwargs)

    def dump_payload(self, obj: t.Any) -> bytes:
        json = super().dump_payload(obj)
        is_compressed = False
        compressed = zlib.compress(json)

        if len(compressed) < (len(json) - 1):
            json = compressed
            is_compressed = True

        base64d = base64_encode(json)

        if is_compressed:
            base64d = b"." + base64d

        return base64d


class URLSafeSerializer(URLSafeSerializerMixin, Serializer[str]):
    """Works like :class:`.Serializer` but dumps and loads into a URL
    safe string consisting of the upper and lowercase character of the
    alphabet as well as ``'_'``, ``'-'`` and ``'.'``.
    """


class URLSafeTimedSerializer(URLSafeSerializerMixin, TimedSerializer[str]):
    """Works like :class:`.TimedSerializer` but dumps and loads into a
    URL safe string consisting of the upper and lowercase character of
    the alphabet as well as ``'_'``, ``'-'`` and ``'.'``.
    """

Write unit test with pytest for method load_payload of class URLSafeSerializerMixin with the following signature: `def test_URLSafeSerializerMixin_load_payload():`
